The emerging paradigm of Green AI challenges this by recognising energy consumption as a critical performance dimension. Despite the high computational demands of automated planning, its energy efficiency has received little attention. This gap is particularly salient given the modular planning structure, in which domain models are specified independently of algorithms. On the other hand, this separation also enables systematic analysis of energy usage through domain model design. We empirically investigate how domain model characteristics affect the energy consumption of classical planners. We introduce a domain model configuration framework that enables controlled variation of features, such as element ordering, action arity, and dead-end states. Using five benchmark domains and five state-of-the-art planners, we analyse energy and runtime impacts across 32 domain variants per benchmark. Results demonstrate that domain-level modifications produce measurable energy differences across planners, with energy consumption not always correlating with runtime.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Decentralized LLM Collaboration with Multi-Agent Actor Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21972)
*Shuo Liu,Tianle Chen,Ryan Amiri,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种多智能体演员-评论家方法（CoLLM-CC和CoLLM-DC）来优化去中心化LLM协作，相比蒙特卡洛方法在长视野或稀疏奖励任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL微调方法依赖预定义执行协议且通常需要集中式执行，而去中心化LLM协作更具实际应用价值。当前方法使用蒙特卡洛方法进行微调，存在高方差问题，需要更多样本才能有效训练。

Method: 提出两种多智能体演员-评论家方法：CoLLM-CC（集中式评论家）和CoLLM-DC（去中心化评论家），用于优化去中心化LLM协作。

Result: 在写作、编码和游戏领域实验中，蒙特卡洛方法和CoLLM-DC在短视野和密集奖励设置中能达到与CoLLM-CC相当的性能。但在长视野或稀疏奖励任务中，两者均表现不佳：蒙特卡洛方法需要更多样本，CoLLM-DC难以收敛。

Conclusion: 集中式评论家的MAAC方法（CoLLM-CC）在长视野或稀疏奖励任务中具有显著优势，而蒙特卡洛方法和去中心化评论家方法在短视野密集奖励任务中表现相当。

Abstract: Recent work has explored optimizing LLM collaboration through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). However, most MARL fine-tuning approaches rely on predefined execution protocols, which often require centralized execution. Decentralized LLM collaboration is more appealing in practice, as agents can run inference in parallel with flexible deployments. Also, current approaches use Monte Carlo methods for fine-tuning, which suffer from high variance and thus require more samples to train effectively. Actor-critic methods are prevalent in MARL for dealing with these issues, so we developed Multi-Agent Actor-Critic (MAAC) methods to optimize decentralized LLM collaboration. In this paper, we analyze when and why these MAAC methods are beneficial. We propose 2 MAAC approaches, \textbf{CoLLM-CC} with a \textbf{C}entralized \textbf{C}ritic and \textbf{CoLLM-DC} with \textbf{D}ecentralized \textbf{C}ritics. Our experiments across writing, coding, and game-playing domains show that Monte Carlo methods and CoLLM-DC can achieve performance comparable to CoLLM-CC in short-horizon and dense-reward settings. However, they both underperform CoLLM-CC on long-horizon or sparse-reward tasks, where Monte Carlo methods require substantially more samples and CoLLM-DC struggles to converge. Our code is available at https://github.com/OpenMLRL/CoMLRL/releases/tag/v1.3.2.

</details>


### [118] [Mind the Gap: How Elicitation Protocols Shape the Stated-Revealed Preference Gap in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21975)
*Pranav Mahajan,Ihor Kendiukhov,Syed Hussain,Lydia Nottingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了语言模型中陈述偏好与显示偏好之间的相关性如何受评估协议影响，发现相关性高度依赖协议设计，特别是允许中立和弃权选项会显著改变结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要依赖二元强制选择提示，这会将真实偏好与评估协议的人工因素混为一谈。需要系统研究不同评估协议如何影响语言模型中陈述偏好与显示偏好的相关性。

Method: 研究了24个语言模型，通过允许中立和弃权选项来改进陈述偏好评估，并测试了系统提示引导对显示偏好的影响。使用AIRiskDilemmas数据集进行实验。

Result: 1) 允许中立和弃权排除弱信号后，陈述偏好与强制选择显示偏好的Spearman秩相关显著改善；2) 进一步允许显示偏好中的弃权使相关性降至接近零或负值；3) 使用陈述偏好进行系统提示引导未能可靠改善AIRiskDilemmas上的相关性。

Conclusion: 陈述偏好与显示偏好的相关性高度依赖评估协议设计，偏好评估需要能够处理不确定偏好的方法，简单的二元强制选择协议存在局限性。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a stated-revealed (SvR) preference gap in language models (LMs): a mismatch between the values models endorse and the choices they make in context. Existing evaluations rely heavily on binary forced-choice prompting, which entangles genuine preferences with artifacts of the elicitation protocol. We systematically study how elicitation protocols affect SvR correlation across 24 LMs. Allowing neutrality and abstention during stated preference elicitation allows us to exclude weak signals, substantially improving Spearman's rank correlation ($ρ$) between volunteered stated preferences and forced-choice revealed preferences. However, further allowing abstention in revealed preferences drives $ρ$ to near-zero or negative values due to high neutrality rates. Finally, we find that system prompt steering using stated preferences during revealed preference elicitation does not reliably improve SvR correlation on AIRiskDilemmas. Together, our results show that SvR correlation is highly protocol-dependent and that preference elicitation requires methods that account for indeterminate preferences.

</details>


### [119] [VERSA: Verified Event Data Format for Reliable Soccer Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21981)
*Geonhee Jo,Mingu Kang,Kangmin Lee,Minho Lee,Pascal Bauer,Sang-Ki Ko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERSA是一个用于足球事件数据验证的系统框架，通过状态转移模型检测和纠正逻辑不一致性，显著提升数据质量和下游分析可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流数据在体育分析等领域至关重要，但现有数据存在逻辑不一致性（如事件顺序错误或缺失事件）等质量问题，限制了分析模型的可靠性。特别是在足球领域，这些问题影响了球员贡献量化和战术模式识别等细粒度分析的准确性。

Method: 提出VERSA（Verified Event Data Format for Reliable Soccer Analytics）验证框架，基于状态转移模型定义有效事件序列，实现事件流数据中异常模式的自动检测和纠正。该框架能够确保足球事件数据的完整性。

Result: 对K League 1（2024赛季）Bepro提供的事件数据检查发现，18.81%的记录事件存在逻辑不一致性。VERSA显著提高了跨数据提供者的一致性，确保异构数据源的稳定统一表示。经VERSA精炼的数据显著提升了下游任务VAEP（评估球员贡献）的鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 验证过程能有效提高数据驱动分析的可靠性。VERSA框架为足球事件数据质量提供了系统性解决方案，解决了现有数据中的逻辑不一致性问题，为可靠的细粒度分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Event stream data is a critical resource for fine-grained analysis across various domains, including financial transactions, system operations, and sports. In sports, it is actively used for fine-grained analyses such as quantifying player contributions and identifying tactical patterns. However, the reliability of these models is fundamentally limited by inherent data quality issues that cause logical inconsistencies (e.g., incorrect event ordering or missing events). To this end, this study proposes VERSA (Verified Event Data Format for Reliable Soccer Analytics), a systematic verification framework that ensures the integrity of event stream data within the soccer domain. VERSA is based on a state-transition model that defines valid event sequences, thereby enabling the automatic detection and correction of anomalous patterns within the event stream data. Notably, our examination of event data from the K League 1 (2024 season), provided by Bepro, detected that 18.81% of all recorded events exhibited logical inconsistencies. Addressing such integrity issues, our experiments demonstrate that VERSA significantly enhances cross-provider consistency, ensuring stable and unified data representation across heterogeneous sources. Furthermore, we demonstrate that data refined by VERSA significantly improves the robustness and performance of a downstream task called VAEP, which evaluates player contributions. These results highlight that the verification process is highly effective in increasing the reliability of data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [120] [Liquid Interfaces: A Dynamic Ontology for the Interoperability of Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21993)
*Dhiogo de Sá,Carlos Schmiedel,Carlos Pereira Lopes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"液态接口"协调范式，将接口从静态技术制品转变为运行时通过意图表达和语义协商产生的短暂关系事件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件架构难以支持具有自适应、概率性和上下文依赖推理能力的自主智能体，而系统集成仍被静态接口和确定性契约主导

Method: 引入液态接口协调范式，形式化该模型，提出液态接口协议(LIP)来管理意图驱动交互、协商执行和在语义不确定性下强制执行短暂性，并描述参考架构

Result: 液态接口为基于智能体的系统提供自适应协调的原则性基础，展示了实际可行性

Conclusion: 液态接口通过将接口重新概念化为短暂关系事件，为自主智能体系统的自适应协调提供了新的范式，解决了静态接口与动态智能体需求之间的不匹配问题

Abstract: Contemporary software architectures struggle to support autonomous agents whose reasoning is adaptive, probabilistic, and context-dependent, while system integration remains dominated by static interfaces and deterministic contracts. This paper introduces Liquid Interfaces, a coordination paradigm in which interfaces are not persistent technical artifacts, but ephemeral relational events that emerge through intention articulation and semantic negotiation at runtime.We formalize this model and present the Liquid Interface Protocol (LIP),which governs intention-driven interaction, negotiated execution, and enforce ephemerality under semantic uncertainty. We further discuss the governance implications of this approach and describe a reference architecture that demonstrates practical feasibility. Liquid Interfaces provide a principled foundation for adaptive coordination in agent-based systems

</details>


### [121] [Exploring Reasoning Reward Model for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22154)
*Kaixuan Fan,Kaituo Feng,Manyuan Zhang,Tianshuo Peng,Zhixun Li,Yilei Jiang,Shuang Chen,Peng Pei,Xunliang Cai,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent-RRM奖励模型，为智能体推理轨迹提供结构化反馈（推理轨迹、针对性批评、总体评分），并通过三种集成策略提升智能体强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习方法主要依赖稀疏的结果奖励，无法区分中间推理质量，导致训练效果欠佳。需要更细粒度的反馈机制来指导智能体改进推理过程。

Method: 提出Agent Reasoning Reward Model (Agent-RRM)，包含三个组件：显式推理轨迹、针对性批评（突出推理缺陷）、总体评分。开发三种集成策略：Reagent-C（文本增强精炼）、Reagent-R（奖励增强指导）、Reagent-U（统一反馈集成）。

Result: 在12个多样化基准测试中，Reagent-U表现最佳，在GAIA上达到43.7%，在WebWalkerQA上达到46.2%，验证了推理奖励模型和训练方案的有效性。

Conclusion: Agent-RRM通过提供结构化反馈显著提升了智能体强化学习的性能，为智能体推理训练提供了更有效的监督机制，代码、模型和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) has achieved notable success in enabling agents to perform complex reasoning and tool use. However, most methods still relies on sparse outcome-based reward for training. Such feedback fails to differentiate intermediate reasoning quality, leading to suboptimal training results. In this paper, we introduce Agent Reasoning Reward Model (Agent-RRM), a multi-faceted reward model that produces structured feedback for agentic trajectories, including (1) an explicit reasoning trace , (2) a focused critique that provides refinement guidance by highlighting reasoning flaws, and (3) an overall score that evaluates process performance. Leveraging these signals, we systematically investigate three integration strategies: Reagent-C (text-augmented refinement), Reagent-R (reward-augmented guidance), and Reagent-U (unified feedback integration). Extensive evaluations across 12 diverse benchmarks demonstrate that Reagent-U yields substantial performance leaps, achieving 43.7% on GAIA and 46.2% on WebWalkerQA, validating the effectiveness of our reasoning reward model and training schemes. Code, models, and datasets are all released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [122] [CAR-bench: Evaluating the Consistency and Limit-Awareness of LLM Agents under Real-World Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22027)
*Johannes Kirmayr,Lukas Stappen,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAR-bench：针对车载语音助手场景的LLM智能体基准测试，重点评估一致性、不确定性处理和能力认知，包含幻觉任务和消歧任务，揭示现有模型在真实场景中的可靠性不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准测试主要关注理想化环境下的任务完成度，忽视了在真实用户场景中的可靠性。车载语音助手等应用中，用户常提出不完整或模糊请求，智能体需要通过对话、工具使用和策略遵守来管理内在不确定性，需要更全面的评估框架

Method: 引入CAR-bench基准测试，包含LLM模拟用户、领域策略和58个互联工具（导航、生产力、充电、车辆控制）。除了标准任务完成度，还设计了幻觉任务（测试在工具或信息缺失时的极限认知）和消歧任务（要求通过澄清或内部信息收集解决不确定性）

Result: 基线结果显示，所有任务类型上偶尔成功与持续成功之间存在巨大差距。即使是前沿推理LLM在消歧任务上的持续通过率也低于50%（因过早行动），在幻觉任务中经常违反策略或编造信息以满足用户请求，突显了真实场景中LLM智能体需要更高的可靠性和自我认知

Conclusion: CAR-bench揭示了现有LLM智能体在真实世界用户场景中的可靠性不足，特别是在处理不确定性和认知自身能力极限方面。该基准为开发更可靠、自我认知的LLM智能体提供了重要评估框架，强调了在车载助手等关键应用中改进模型可靠性的必要性

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for Large Language Model (LLM) agents focus on task completion under idealistic settings but overlook reliability in real-world, user-facing applications. In domains, such as in-car voice assistants, users often issue incomplete or ambiguous requests, creating intrinsic uncertainty that agents must manage through dialogue, tool use, and policy adherence. We introduce CAR-bench, a benchmark for evaluating consistency, uncertainty handling, and capability awareness in multi-turn, tool-using LLM agents in an in-car assistant domain. The environment features an LLM-simulated user, domain policies, and 58 interconnected tools spanning navigation, productivity, charging, and vehicle control. Beyond standard task completion, CAR-bench introduces Hallucination tasks that test agents' limit-awareness under missing tools or information, and Disambiguation tasks that require resolving uncertainty through clarification or internal information gathering. Baseline results reveal large gaps between occasional and consistent success on all task types. Even frontier reasoning LLMs achieve less than 50% consistent pass rate on Disambiguation tasks due to premature actions, and frequently violate policies or fabricate information to satisfy user requests in Hallucination tasks, underscoring the need for more reliable and self-aware LLM agents in real-world settings.

</details>


### [123] [Defining Operational Conditions for Safety-Critical AI-Based Systems from Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22118)
*Johann Christensen,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多维度核表示的安全设计方法，从已有数据中后验定义操作设计域，用于数据驱动的安全关键AI系统认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键AI系统开发中，传统ODD定义依赖于早期专家知识和标准，但真实世界复杂系统或已有数据情况下，环境条件定义极具挑战，导致ODD描述不完整，而认证又要求明确的ODD定义。

Method: 提出安全设计方法，使用多维度核表示从先前收集的数据中后验定义ODD，通过蒙特卡洛方法和真实航空用例验证，并定义两个ODD相等的条件。

Result: 验证表明数据驱动的ODD能够等于原始隐藏的ODD，该方法为数据驱动的安全关键AI系统未来认证提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 基于核的安全设计ODD方法能够支持数据驱动的安全关键AI系统认证，解决了传统ODD定义在复杂现实环境中的局限性问题。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) has been on the rise in many domains, including numerous safety-critical applications. However, for complex systems found in the real world, or when data already exist, defining the underlying environmental conditions is extremely challenging. This often results in an incomplete description of the environment in which the AI-based system must operate. Nevertheless, this description, called the Operational Design Domain (ODD), is required in many domains for the certification of AI-based systems. Traditionally, the ODD is created in the early stages of the development process, drawing on sophisticated expert knowledge and related standards. This paper presents a novel Safety-by-Design method to a posteriori define the ODD from previously collected data using a multi-dimensional kernel-based representation. This approach is validated through both Monte Carlo methods and a real-world aviation use case for a future safety-critical collision-avoidance system. Moreover, by defining under what conditions two ODDs are equal, the paper shows that the data-driven ODD can equal the original, underlying hidden ODD of the data. Utilizing the novel, Safe-by-Design kernel-based ODD enables future certification of data-driven, safety-critical AI-based systems.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [The augmented NLP bound for maximum-entropy remote sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20970)
*Gabriel Ponte,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出最大熵远程采样问题（MERSP）的改进上界方法，包括增强NLP上界和有效的对角缩放技术，在理论保证和数值实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MERSP问题中现有上界方法（NLP上界和谱上界）的局限性，特别是需要处理秩亏协方差矩阵的情况，并提高上界的紧致性。

Method: 1) 建立NLP上界和谱上界的支配关系；2) 提出基于微妙凸松弛的"增强NLP上界"；3) 推导秩亏协方差矩阵的上界；4) 引入使用正参数向量的有效对角缩放技术。

Result: 1) 建立了两种现有上界之间的支配关系；2) 增强NLP上界在满足特定条件下严格优于普通NLP上界；3) 能够处理秩亏协方差矩阵；4) 对角缩放技术在基准实例上显著提高了上界质量。

Conclusion: 提出的增强NLP上界和对角缩放技术显著推进了MERSP上界计算的技术水平，特别是在理论保证和数值性能方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: The maximum-entropy remote sampling problem (MERSP) is to select a subset of s random variables from a set of n random variables, so as to maximize the information concerning a set of target random variables that are not directly observable. We assume throughout that the set of all of these random variables follows a joint Gaussian distribution, and that we have the covariance matrix available. Finally, we measure information using Shannon's differential entropy.
  The main approach for exact solution of moderate-sized instances of MERSP has been branch-and-bound, and so previous work concentrated on upper bounds. Prior to our work, there were two upper-bounding methods for MERSP: the so-called NLP bound and the spectral bound, both introduced 25 years ago. We are able now to establish domination results between these two upper bounds. We propose an ``augmented NLP bound'' based on a subtle convex relaxation. We provide theoretical guarantees, giving sufficient conditions under which the augmented NLP bound strictly dominates the ordinary NLP bound. In addition, the augmented NLP formulation allows us to derive upper bounds for rank-deficient covariance matrices when they satisfy a technical condition. This is in contrast to the earlier work on the ordinary NLP bound that worked with only positive definite covariance matrices. Finally, we introduce a novel and very effective diagonal-scaling technique for MERSP, employing a positive vector of parameters. Numerical experiments on benchmark instances demonstrate the effectiveness of our approaches in advancing the state of the art for calculating upper bounds on MERSP.

</details>


### [125] [Thompson Sampling Algorithm for Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20973)
*Asaf Cohen,Ruolan He,Yuqiong Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有N个竞争玩家的线性二次随机微分博弈，包含未知公共漂移矩阵，使用贝叶斯滤波和Thompson采样算法，获得O(√TlogT)的贝叶斯遗憾界，算法收敛到纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有未知公共漂移矩阵的多玩家随机微分博弈问题，传统方法难以处理未知参数，需要开发能够同时学习参数并实现均衡策略的算法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯方法，假设漂移矩阵具有高斯先验，使用滤波技术更新后验估计；提出基于Thompson采样的算法，具有动态回合长度，用于近似均衡策略。

Result: 证明每个玩家的贝叶斯遗憾具有O(√TlogT)的误差界，与玩家数量无关；平均单位时间遗憾趋于零；算法最终收敛到纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 提出的Thompson采样算法能够有效处理具有未知公共漂移的线性二次随机微分博弈，在保证遗憾界的同时实现纳什均衡，为多玩家强化学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study a stochastic differential game with $N$ competitive players in a linear-quadratic framework with ergodic cost, where $d$-dimensional diffusion processes govern the state dynamics with an unknown common drift (matrix). Assuming a Gaussian prior on the drift, we use filtering techniques to update its posterior estimates. Based on these estimates, we propose a Thompson-sampling-based algorithm with dynamic episode lengths to approximate strategies. We show that the Bayesian regret for each player has an error bound of order $O(\sqrt{T\log(T)})$, where $T$ is the time-horizon, independent of the number of players. This implies that average regret per unit time goes to zero. Finally, we prove that the algorithm results in a Nash equilibrium.

</details>


### [126] [The dual-path fixing strategy and its application to the set-covering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20977)
*Paulo Michel F. Yamagishi,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出双路径固定策略，利用对偶算法求解混合整数非线性优化问题的松弛问题，旨在比基于单一对偶可行解的固定策略更强大，同时比强固定策略快得多


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分支定界算法中应用对偶算法求解松弛问题，最终目标是提升分支定界算法的整体性能。研究动机在于开发比传统基于单一对偶可行解的变量固定策略更有效，同时比计算代价高的强固定策略更快的替代方案

Method: 引入双路径固定策略，利用对偶算法求解混合整数非线性优化问题的松弛问题。该方法比基于单一对偶可行解的固定策略（如混合整数线性优化中的标准约化成本固定）更强大，但比强固定策略快得多，基本上只需要对偶算法本身的时间

Result: 在文献中的混合整数线性优化集合覆盖实例上成功测试了该方法，使用对偶单纯形法求解连续松弛问题。结果表明该方法在提升分支定界算法性能方面具有潜力

Conclusion: 双路径固定策略为分支定界算法提供了一种有效的变量固定方法，既比传统基于单一对偶可行解的固定策略更强大，又比计算代价高的强固定策略更高效，有望显著提升混合整数优化问题的求解性能

Abstract: We introduce the dual-path fixing strategy to exploit dual algorithms for solving relaxations of mixed-integer nonlinear-optimization problems. Such dual algorithms are naturally applied in the context of branch-and-bound, and eventual impact on the success of branch-and-bound is our strong motivation. Our fixing strategy aims to be more powerful than the common strategy of fixing variables based on a single dual-feasible solution (e.g., standard reduced-cost fixing for mixed-integer linear optimization), but to be much faster than ``strong fixing'', essentially requiring no more time than that of the dual algorithm that we exploit. We have successfully tested our ideas on mixed-integer linear-optimization set-covering instances from the literature, in the context of the dual-simplex method applied to the continuous relaxations.

</details>


### [127] [Noisy Pairwise-Comparison Random Search for Smooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21166)
*Taha El Bakkali,Rayane Bouftini,Qiuyi Zhang,Omar Saadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NCRS方法，通过随机线搜索利用目标函数的固有维度k而非环境维度d，在仅使用噪声成对比较的情况下最小化高维光滑非凸目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零阶方法受限于环境维度d，而许多高维优化问题具有低固有维度结构。仅使用噪声成对比较（而非函数值）的优化场景需要适应固有维度的新方法。

Method: 提出Noisy-Comparison Random Search (NCRS)，一种直接搜索方法，利用随机线搜索适应固有维度k≤d。包含两种变体：在均匀边界oracle下使用标准NCRS；在平局感知噪声模型下使用多数投票变体。

Result: 在均匀边界oracle下，NCRS达到ε-稳定性的复杂度为O(k/(p²ε²))，用固有维度k替代了环境维度d。在平局感知噪声模型下，多数投票变体达到ε-稳定性的复杂度为O(k²/ε⁴)。

Conclusion: NCRS方法能够有效利用目标函数的低固有维度结构，在仅使用噪声成对比较的情况下实现高效的非凸优化，显著降低了维度依赖。

Abstract: We consider minimizing high-dimensional smooth nonconvex objectives using only noisy pairwise comparisons. Unlike classical zeroth-order methods limited by the ambient dimension $d$, we propose Noisy-Comparison Random Search (NCRS), a direct-search method that exploits random line search to adapt to the intrinsic dimension $k \le d$. We establish novel non-convex convergence guarantees for approximate stationarity: under a uniform-margin oracle, NCRS attains $ε$-stationarity with complexity $\mathcal{O}(k/(p^{2}ε^{2}))$, explicitly replacing ambient dependence with the intrinsic dimension. Furthermore, we introduce a general tie-aware noise model where comparison quality degrades near ties; for this setting, we prove that a majority-vote variant of NCRS achieves $ε$-stationarity with complexity $\mathcal{O}(k^{2}/ε^{4})$.

</details>


### [128] [Solving the Offline and Online Min-Max Problem of Non-smooth Submodular-Concave Functions: A Zeroth-Order Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21243)
*Amir Ali Farzin,Yuen-Man Pun,Philipp Braun,Tyler Summers,Iman Shames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对非光滑、次模-凹函数的零阶优化方法，用于求解min-max和max-min问题，在离线和在线场景下分别证明了收敛性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决目标函数可能非光滑、对最小化变量具有次模性、对最大化变量具有凹性的min-max和max-min问题。这类问题在机器学习和优化中常见，但现有方法难以处理非光滑和次模特性。

Method: 提出零阶优化方法：对于最小化变量，使用目标函数Lovász扩展的次梯度；对于最大化变量，使用高斯平滑估计平滑函数的梯度。该方法结合了次模优化和随机优化的技术。

Result: 理论证明：在离线情况下，算法期望收敛到ε-鞍点；在在线情况下，算法期望达到O(√(N·P̄_N))的在线对偶间隙，其中N是迭代次数，P̄_N是最优决策序列的路径长度。提供了复杂度分析和超参数选择。

Conclusion: 提出的零阶方法能有效处理非光滑、次模-凹函数的min-max问题，在离线和在线场景下都有理论保证，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We consider max-min and min-max problems with objective functions that are possibly non-smooth, submodular with respect to the minimiser and concave with respect to the maximiser. We investigate the performance of a zeroth-order method applied to this problem. The method is based on the subgradient of the Lovász extension of the objective function with respect to the minimiser and based on Gaussian smoothing to estimate the smoothed function gradient with respect to the maximiser. In expectation sense, we prove the convergence of the algorithm to an $ε$-saddle point in the offline case. Moreover, we show that, in the expectation sense, in the online setting, the algorithm achieves $O(\sqrt{N\bar{P}_N})$ online duality gap, where $N$ is the number of iterations and $\bar{P}_N$ is the path length of the sequence of optimal decisions. The complexity analysis and hyperparameter selection are presented for all the cases. The theoretical results are illustrated via numerical examples.

</details>


### [129] [Certifying optimality in nonconvex robust PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21333)
*Pinxi Gong,Lexiao Lai,Jianhao Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了鲁棒主成分分析中基于低秩因子分解的非光滑非凸优化问题，证明了在标准不相干条件和随机腐败模型下，真实秩r矩阵的因子分解解具有特定的临界点性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒主成分分析旨在从具有稀疏腐败的完全观测数据中恢复低秩矩阵。传统方法使用非光滑非凸目标函数（最小化逐项绝对残差）拟合低秩因子分解，但这类优化问题的理论性质尚未完全理解，特别是其临界点特性和局部几何结构。

Method: 采用低秩因子分解方法，最小化逐项绝对残差（L1损失）。在标准不相干条件和腐败支撑的随机模型假设下，分析真实秩r矩阵的因子分解解。研究两种情形：因子秩等于r和因子秩超过r的情况。

Result: 1. 在标准不相干条件和随机腐败模型下，以高概率，真实秩r矩阵的每个因子分解都是Clarke临界点。
2. 当因子秩等于r时，这些解是尖锐局部最小值。
3. 当因子秩超过r时，这些解是严格鞍点。

Conclusion: 该研究为鲁棒主成分分析的非光滑非凸优化问题提供了理论保证，揭示了真实解在因子分解空间中的几何特性：在正确秩设定下是尖锐局部最小值，在超参数化设定下是严格鞍点，这为算法设计和收敛性分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Robust principal component analysis seeks to recover a low-rank matrix from fully observed data with sparse corruptions. A scalable approach fits a low-rank factorization by minimizing the sum of entrywise absolute residuals, leading to a nonsmooth and nonconvex objective. Under standard incoherence conditions and a random model for the corruption support, we study factorizations of the ground-truth rank-$r$ matrix with both factors of rank $r$. With high probability, every such factorization is a Clarke critical point. We also characterize the local geometry: when the factorization rank equals $r$, these solutions are sharp local minima; when it exceeds $r$, they are strict saddle points.

</details>


### [130] [Decentralized Learning with Dynamically Refined Edge Weights: A Data-Dependent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21355)
*Rongxing Du,Hoi-To Wai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出D3GD框架，通过数据依赖的动态有向图边权重设计加速去中心化优化，相比传统Di-DGD算法提升30-40%收敛速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有向图去中心化梯度下降(Di-DGD)算法在边权重设计上缺乏数据适应性，限制了优化收敛速度。需要开发能够根据数据特征动态调整通信策略的框架来加速去中心化优化

Method: 提出动态有向去中心化梯度(D3GD)框架，从Di-DGD的新收敛分析中推导出成本函数启发式设计函数，实现数据依赖的边权重动态设计。开发完全去中心化版本，每个智能体仅使用邻居信息优化通信策略

Result: D3GD相比Di-DGD加速收敛30-40%达到平稳解，学习到的边权重能够自适应数据相似性，为有向图上的去中心化学习提供了实用替代方案

Conclusion: 数据依赖的边权重动态设计是加速去中心化优化的有效策略，D3GD框架通过自适应通信策略显著提升收敛性能，为有向图分布式学习提供了新方向

Abstract: This paper aims to accelerate decentralized optimization by strategically designing the edge weights used in the agent-to-agent message exchanges. We propose a Dynamic Directed Decentralized Gradient (D3GD) framework and show that the proposed data-dependent framework is a practical alternative to the classical directed DGD (Di-DGD) algorithm for learning on directed graphs. To obtain a strategy for edge weights refinement, we derive a design function inspired by the cost-to-go function in a new convergence analysis for Di-DGD. This results in a data-dependent dynamical design for the edge weights. A fully decentralized version of D3GD is developed such that each agent refines its communication strategy using only neighbor's information. Numerical experiments show that D3GD accelerates convergence towards stationary solution by 30-40\% over Di-DGD, and learns edge weights that adapt to data similarity.

</details>


### [131] [Manifold constrained steepest descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21487)
*Kaiwei Yang,Lexiao Lai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出MCSD（流形约束最速下降法），一种单循环优化框架，通过LMO选择范数诱导的最速下降方向，然后通过投影返回流形，解决了现有LMO方法在流形约束问题中需要嵌套循环的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于范数约束线性最小化oracle（LMO）的优化器（如谱梯度下降和Muon）在大规模学习中很有吸引力，但将其扩展到流形约束问题很困难，通常会导致需要迭代求解切空间子问题的嵌套循环方案。

Method: 提出MCSD框架：1）通过应用于黎曼梯度的LMO选择范数诱导的最速下降方向；2）通过投影返回流形。还提出了SPEL，这是MCSD在Stiefel流形上的谱范数特化，通过快速矩阵符号计算实现可扩展实现。

Result: 在标准光滑性假设下，建立了MCSD及其随机动量变体的收敛保证。在PCA、正交约束CNN和流形约束LLM适配器调优实验中，相对于标准黎曼基线和现有流形感知LMO方法，显示出改进的稳定性和竞争性能。

Conclusion: MCSD提供了一个高效的单循环框架，用于流形约束优化，避免了现有LMO方法的嵌套循环问题，在多个实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Norm-constrained linear minimization oracle (LMO)-based optimizers such as spectral gradient descent and Muon are attractive in large-scale learning, but extending them to manifold-constrained problems is nontrivial and often leads to nested-loop schemes that solve tangent-space subproblems iteratively. We propose \emph{Manifold Constrained Steepest Descent} (MCSD), a single-loop framework for optimization over manifolds that selects a norm-induced steepest-descent direction via an LMO applied to the Riemannian gradient, and then returns to the manifold via projection. Under standard smoothness assumptions, we establish convergence guarantees for MCSD and a stochastic momentum variant. We further introduce \emph{SPEL}, the spectral-norm specialization of MCSD on the Stiefel manifold, which admits scalable implementations via fast matrix sign computations. Experiments on PCA, orthogonality-constrained CNNs, and manifold-constrained LLM adapter tuning demonstrate improved stability and competitive performance relative to standard Riemannian baselines and existing manifold-aware LMO methods.

</details>


### [132] [A data-based image representation for continuous-time LTI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21606)
*Amine Othmane,Philipp Schmitz,Karl Worthmann,Kathrin Flaßkamp*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据的线性系统图像表示方法，利用连续时间基本引理，避免求解微分代数方程，使用代数微分器近似导数，显著降低复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据的方法需要求解微分代数方程，计算复杂且对测量噪声敏感。需要开发更稳定、计算效率更高的数据驱动系统表示方法

Method: 利用连续时间版本的Willems基本引理，提出图像表示方法避免求解微分代数方程，使用代数微分器近似导数，消除冗余自由度减少待识别未知量

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法有效，即使在严重测量干扰下也能稳定工作，显著降低了数据驱动表示的复杂度

Conclusion: 提出的图像表示方法为线性系统数据驱动控制提供了更稳定、计算效率更高的替代方案，特别适用于存在测量噪声的实际应用

Abstract: We derive a numerically stable method to obtain an image representation of an unknown linear system only from data, leveraging a continuous-time version of Willems et al.'s fundamental lemma. We propose a data-based representation that, unlike previous approaches, avoids solving differential-algebraic equations and uses derivatives approximated by algebraic differentiators. Our image-based formulation significantly reduces the complexity of the data-driven representation by eliminating redundant degrees of freedom and thus reducing the number of unknown quantities to be identified. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed approach, even in the presence of severe measurement disturbances.

</details>


### [133] [Outrunning the Omega Clock: A Singular Control Problem for Dividend Optimisation with Ruin and Time-in-Distress Default](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21705)
*Andi Bodnariu,Nils Engler,Neofytos Rodosthenous*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文扩展了经典股息问题，引入基于占用时间的路径依赖违约机制，当盈余低于困境阈值的时间过长时触发违约，即使从未达到零。通过奇异随机控制问题求解，识别出三类最优股息策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统股息问题中破产仅发生在盈余首次达到零时，这未能充分反映现实中的财务困境。现实中，即使公司从未达到零盈余，长期处于低流动性或资本不足状态也可能触发监管干预或运营失败。因此需要更现实的违约机制来捕捉财务困境。

Method: 提出基于占用时间的路径依赖违约机制，当盈余低于特定困境阈值的时间累积超过一定限度时触发违约。将问题建模为具有不连续状态依赖贴现和终止的奇异随机控制问题。采用定制的顺序猜测-验证方法进行解析求解。

Result: 完全解析解识别出三类最优股息策略，对应双重破产结构的不同参数机制。在某些困境阈值下，最优策略表现出断开的行为区域和非行为区域。与经典股息问题不同，由困境阈值以下占用时间引起的更高有效贴现可能导致股息支付延迟而非提前。

Conclusion: 基于占用时间的违约机制为股息问题提供了更现实的财务困境建模方法。该模型揭示了传统模型中未观察到的复杂最优策略结构，特别是困境阈值导致的断开区域和延迟股息支付现象，对财务风险管理具有重要启示。

Abstract: This paper extends the classical dividend problem by incorporating a novel, path-dependent mechanism of firm default. In the traditional framework, ruin occurs when the surplus process first reaches zero. In contrast, default in our model may also arise when the surplus spends an excessive amount of time below a distress threshold, even without ever hitting zero. This occupation-time-based default criterion captures financial distress more realistically, as prolonged periods of low liquidity or capitalisation may trigger regulatory intervention or operational failure. The resulting optimisation problem is formulated as a new singular stochastic control problem with discontinuous state-dependent discounting and killing. We provide a complete analytical solution via a bespoke sequential guess-and-verify method and identify three distinct classes of optimal dividend strategies corresponding to different parameter regimes of the dual-ruin structure. Notably, for certain distress thresholds, the optimal policy features disconnected action and inaction regions. We further show that, unlike in the classical dividend problem, higher effective discounting induced by occupation time below a distress level can lead to delayed, rather than earlier, dividend payments.

</details>


### [134] [On Diagonalizable Systems with Random Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21710)
*Yuan Zhang,Yutong Han,Yuanqing Xia,Aming Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由有向Erdős-Rényi随机图表示的结构化系统中结构可对角化的渐近概率分布，分析了不同边密度区域下的分布特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可对角性在多变量系统分析和设计中具有重要作用。结构可对角化对于验证和优化各种结构系统特性非常有用。研究随机图模型中结构可对角化的概率分布有助于理解结构化系统的统计特性。

Method: 利用最近建立的图论特征化方法，分析不同边密度区域下结构可对角化图的分布。对于稠密图，证明系统几乎总是结构可对角化；对于中等密度图，推导渐近概率的紧上下界；对于极稀疏图，证明概率趋近于0。通过不同顶点数和连接概率的数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 1. 稠密图：系统几乎总是结构可对角化；2. 中等密度图：获得结构可对角化渐近概率的紧上下界；3. 极稀疏图：结构可对角化概率趋近于0；4. 理论结果通过广泛的数值模拟得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了有向Erdős-Rényi随机图中结构可对角化的完整渐近概率分布理论，为结构化系统的统计分析和设计提供了理论基础。不同密度区域的特性分析揭示了结构可对角化概率与图稀疏度的密切关系。

Abstract: Diagonalizability plays an important role in the analysis and design of multivariable systems. A structured matrix is called structurally diagonalizable if almost all of its numerical realizations, obtained by assigning real values to its free entries, are diagonalizable. Structural diagonalizability is useful for the verification and optimization of various structural system properties. In this paper, we study the asymptotic probability distribution of structural diagonalizability for structured systems whose system matrices are represented by directed Erdős-Rényi random graphs. Leveraging a recently established graph-theoretic characterization of structural diagonalizability, we analyze the distribution of structurally diagonalizable graphs under different edge-density regimes. For dense graphs, we prove that the system is almost always structurally diagonalizable. For graphs of medium density, we derive tight upper and lower bounds on the asymptotic probability of structural diagonalizability. For extremely sparse graphs, we show that this probability approaches 0. The theoretical results are validated through extensive numerical simulations with varying numbers of vertices and connection probabilities.

</details>


### [135] [Stochastic Optimal Linear Quadratic Controls with A Recursive Cost Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21748)
*Lin Li,Jiongmin Yong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有递归成本泛函的随机线性二次控制问题，涉及L¹空间中的BSDE，建立了合适的框架并分析了开环和闭环可解性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有递归成本泛函的随机线性二次控制问题，这类问题涉及L¹空间中的BSDE，其适定性是一个微妙问题，需要建立合适的理论框架来正确表述问题

Method: 采用合适的理论框架来正确表述LQ问题，通过前向-后向随机微分方程和Riccati微分方程的可解性来研究开环和闭环可解性

Result: 建立了具有递归成本泛函的随机LQ控制问题的正确表述，证明了开环可解性等价于FBSDE的可解性，闭环可解性等价于Riccati微分方程的可解性

Conclusion: 成功建立了具有递归成本泛函的随机LQ控制问题的理论框架，为这类问题的开环和闭环控制提供了完整的可解性特征

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic linear quadratic (LQ, for short) control problem with a recursive cost functional. It involves BSDEs in $L^1$ whose well-posedness is a subtle issue. A suitable framework has been adopted so that the corresponding LQ problem is correctly formulated. Open-loop and closed-loop solvability of such an LQ problem have been investigated and characterized by the solvability of an FBSDE and that of Riccati differential equation.

</details>


### [136] [ZOBA: An Efficient Single-loop Zeroth-order Bilevel Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21836)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出ZOBA和HF-ZOBA两种单循环有限差分双层优化算法，在零阶设置下实现高效优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双层优化方法需要超梯度信息，但在零阶设置（无法获取一阶信息）下不适用。现有有限差分方法依赖双循环算法，计算成本高且并行性差。

Method: 提出ZOBA（零阶双层逼近）算法，利用基于延迟信息的有限差分超梯度近似，消除嵌套循环需求。进一步提出HF-ZOBA（无Hessian变体），避免Hessian计算。

Result: 在非凸设置下建立收敛率，复杂度为O(p(d+p)²ε⁻²)，优于基于Hessian近似的方法。数值实验显示在合成函数和对抗机器学习任务中达到与SOTA相当的精度，但计算时间更少。

Conclusion: 首次提出单循环有限差分双层优化算法，在零阶设置下提供高效替代方案，特别适用于黑盒优化任务。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization problems consist of minimizing a value function whose evaluation depends on the solution of an inner optimization problem. These problems are typically tackled using first-order methods that require computing the gradient of the value function ({\it the hypergradient}). In several practical settings, however, first-order information is unavailable ({\it zeroth-order setting}), rendering these methods inapplicable. Finite-difference methods provide an alternative by approximating hypergradients using function evaluations along a set of directions. Nevertheless, such surrogates are notoriously expensive, and existing finite-difference bilevel methods rely on two-loop algorithms that are poorly parallelizable. In this work, we propose ZOBA, the first finite-difference single-loop algorithm for bilevel optimization. Our method leverages finite-difference hypergradient approximations based on delayed information to eliminate the need for nested loops. We analyze the proposed algorithm and establish convergence rates in the non-convex setting, achieving a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(p(d + p)^2\varepsilon^{-2})$, where $p$ and $d$ denote the dimension of inner and outer spaces respectively, which is better than prior approaches based on Hessian approximation. We further introduce and analyze HF-ZOBA, a Hessian-free variant that yields additional complexity improvements. Finally, we corroborate our findings with numerical experiments on synthetic functions and a real-world black-box task in adversarial machine learning. Our results show that our methods achieve accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art techniques while requiring less computation time.

</details>


### [137] [When to Match: A Cost-Balancing Principle for Dynamic Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21858)
*Jie Liu,Hailun Zhang,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于成本平衡原则的在线匹配算法CB，在对抗性到达下获得(1+√Γ)竞争比，优于贪婪和阈值策略，并证明黄金比率是基本不确定性代价下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配平台面临即时匹配与延迟匹配的运营困境：现有方法要么计算复杂、依赖分布假设，要么缺乏最坏情况性能保证。需要一种简单、自适应且具有理论保证的算法。

Method: 提出成本平衡(CB)算法：当累积等待成本达到瞬时匹配成本的校准比例时进行匹配。该原则源于流体极限分析，无需分布假设，适应非平稳到达动态。

Result: CB在对抗性到达下获得(1+√Γ)竞争比，其中Γ量化规模经济，保证成本在离线最优解的常数倍内。贪婪和阈值策略在对抗场景下可能产生无界成本。

Conclusion: 成本平衡原则提供了一种简单有效的在线匹配方法，具有理论保证。证明了(√5+1)/2（黄金比率）是在线匹配不确定性的基本代价下界。实验验证了CB在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Matching platforms, from ridesharing to food delivery to competitive gaming, face a fundamental operational dilemma: match agents immediately to minimize waiting costs, or delay to exploit the efficiency gains of thicker markets. Yet computing optimal policies is generally intractable, sophisticated algorithms often rely on restrictive distributional assumptions, and common heuristics lack worst-case performance guarantees. We formulate a versatile framework for multi-sided matching with general state-dependent cost structures and non-stationary arrival dynamics. Central to our approach is a cost-balancing principle: match when accumulated waiting cost reaches a calibrated proportion of instantaneous matching cost. This equilibrium condition emerges from fluid-limit analysis and motivates a simple, adaptive Cost-Balancing (CB) algorithm requiring no distributional assumptions. We prove that CB achieves a competitive ratio of $(1+\sqrtΓ)$ under adversarial arrivals, where $Γ$ quantifies economies of scale, guaranteeing cost within a constant factor of the offline optimum. In contrast, standard greedy and threshold policies can incur unbounded costs in adversarial scenarios. We further establish a universal lower bound of $(\sqrt{5}+1)/2$ (the golden ratio), quantifying the fundamental price of uncertainty in online matching. Experiments on game matchmaking and real-world food delivery data demonstrate practical effectiveness, with CB consistently outperforming industry-standard heuristics.

</details>


### [138] [Pathwise Learning of Stochastic Dynamical Systems with Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21860)
*Nicole Tianjiao Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于变分推理的神经路径估计方法，用于从噪声非线性观测中同时学习随机动力系统的先验SDE和推断后验路径测度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数据中重建和推断随机动力系统是逆问题和统计学习的基本任务。实际应用中数据通常通过噪声和非线性测量间接观测，挑战在于不仅要近似SDE系数，还要同时给定观测进行后验更新推断。

Method: 提出神经路径估计方法：1) 推导解决与路径wise Zakai方程对应的滤波后验路径测度的随机控制问题；2) 构建生成模型，通过受控扩散和相关Radon-Nikodym导数将先验路径测度映射到后验测度；3) 通过观测过程样本路径的摊销，控制通过噪声观测路径的嵌入学习；4) 学习未知先验SDE和控制可以恢复给定观测样本路径的条件路径测度，并学习诱导相同路径测度的相关SDE。

Result: 在非线性动力系统上进行实验，展示了模型学习多模态、混沌或高维系统的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从噪声非线性观测中同时学习随机动力系统的先验动态和后验路径测度，为复杂动力系统的逆问题提供了一种有效的变分推理框架。

Abstract: The reconstruction and inference of stochastic dynamical systems from data is a fundamental task in inverse problems and statistical learning. While surrogate modeling advances computational methods to approximate these dynamics, standard approaches typically require high-fidelity training data. In many practical settings, the data are indirectly observed through noisy and nonlinear measurement. The challenge lies not only in approximating the coefficients of the SDEs, but in simultaneously inferring the posterior updates given the observations. In this work, we present a neural path estimation approach to solve stochastic dynamical systems based on variational inference. We first derive a stochastic control problem that solve filtering posterior path measure corresponding to a pathwise Zakai equation. We then construct a generative model that maps the prior path measure to posterior measure through the controlled diffusion and the associated Randon-Nykodym derivative. Through an amortization of sample paths of the observation process, the control is learned by an embedding of the noisy observation paths. Thus, we learn the unknown prior SDE and the control can recover the conditional path measure given the observation sample paths and we learn an associated SDE which induces the same path measure. In the end, we perform experiments on nonlinear dynamical systems, demonstrating the model's ability to learn multimodal, chaotic, or high dimensional systems.

</details>


### [139] [On Approximate Computation of Critical Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21917)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Georgina Hall*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明即使对非常简单的非凸函数类，近似计算临界点也是NP难的，这与通常认为非凸优化中近似计算临界点是可处理任务的信念相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战非凸优化领域普遍存在的信念，即近似计算临界点通常是可处理的任务。研究者希望揭示即使对于非常简单的非凸函数类，这一任务在计算复杂性上也是困难的。

Method: 通过复杂性理论证明：证明如果存在多项式时间算法能够为常数度（低至三次）的多项式输出梯度范数不超过2^n的近似临界点，则P=NP。还证明了在额外结构假设下（如临界点存在性、唯一性保证，函数有下界等）的近似计算困难性。

Result: 证明了近似计算临界点的计算困难性：1）对于常数度多项式（低至三次），存在多项式时间近似算法将导致P=NP；2）即使在临界点存在性、唯一性保证，函数有下界等额外结构假设下，近似计算临界点仍然是困难的。

Conclusion: 非凸优化中近似计算临界点通常是计算困难的任务，这与该领域普遍持有的信念相反。即使在相对温和的条件下，这一任务在复杂性理论意义下也是难处理的。

Abstract: We show that computing even very coarse approximations of critical points is intractable for simple classes of nonconvex functions. More concretely, we prove that if there exists a polynomial-time algorithm that takes as input a polynomial in $n$ variables of constant degree (as low as three) and outputs a point whose gradient has Euclidean norm at most $2^n$ whenever the polynomial has a critical point, then P=NP. The algorithm is permitted to return an arbitrary point when no critical point exists. We also prove hardness results for approximate computation of critical points under additional structural assumptions, including settings in which existence and uniqueness of a critical point are guaranteed, the function is lower bounded, and approximation is measured in terms of distance to a critical point. Overall, our results stand in contrast to the commonly-held belief that, in nonconvex optimization, approximate computation of critical points is a tractable task.

</details>


### [140] [Batched First-Order Methods for Parallel LP Solving in MIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21990)
*Nicolas Blin,Stefano Gualandi,Christopher Maes,Andrea Lodi,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种在GPU上并行求解多个线性规划的批处理一阶方法，扩展了原始-对偶混合梯度算法，特别适用于混合整数规划中的强分支和边界收紧等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数规划技术（如强分支和边界收紧）需要并行求解大量相关的线性规划问题。传统基于单纯形法的求解器在GPU上效率不高，需要开发能够充分利用GPU并行计算能力的新方法。

Method: 扩展原始-对偶混合梯度算法，采用矩阵-矩阵运算替代重复的矩阵-向量运算，实现多个线性规划问题的并行批处理求解，专门针对GPU架构优化。

Result: 该方法在GPU上获得了显著的计算优势，通过多个案例研究验证了有效性，并确定了在不同计算环境下，一阶方法优于传统单纯形求解器的问题规模范围。

Conclusion: 这是整数规划算法设计中充分利用GPU能力的重要进展，论证了应将特定操作完全分配给GPU执行，而非在CPU上使用轻量级启发式方法。

Abstract: We present a batched first-order method for solving multiple linear programs in parallel on GPUs. Our approach extends the primal-dual hybrid gradient algorithm to efficiently solve batches of related linear programming problems that arise in mixed-integer programming techniques such as strong branching and bound tightening. By leveraging matrix-matrix operations instead of repeated matrix-vector operations, we obtain significant computational advantages on GPU architectures. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on various case studies and identify the problem sizes where first-order methods outperform traditional simplex-based solvers depending on the computational environment one can use. This is a significant step for the design and development of integer programming algorithms tightly exploiting GPU capabilities where we argue that some specific operations should be allocated to GPUs and performed in full instead of using light-weight heuristic approaches on CPUs.

</details>


### [141] [Anderson Mixing in Bures Wasserstein Space of Gaussian Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22038)
*Vitalii Aksenov,Martin Eigel,Mathias Oster*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于高斯分布的黎曼安德森混合（RAM）方法，通过重用残差历史来加速固定点迭代，在Bures-Wasserstein流形上实现显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多统计任务（如采样或计算Wasserstein重心）可以重新表述为概率分布上的算子固定点问题。加速标准固定点迭代方案为设计这些问题的有效数值方法提供了新途径。Wasserstein几何虽然不完全黎曼，但允许定义有用的黎曼概念，这启发了黎曼数值方法的适配。

Method: 开发并实现了高斯分布的黎曼安德森混合（RAM）方法。该方法重用残差历史来改进迭代复杂度，相比Picard方法额外成本可忽略。证明了Bures-Wasserstein流形上的某些开球满足RAM收敛要求。

Result: 数值实验显示，与Picard迭代相比，RAM方法实现了显著加速，性能与黎曼梯度下降和共轭梯度方法相当。

Conclusion: RAM方法为高斯分布上的固定点问题提供了有效的加速方案，通过利用Wasserstein几何的黎曼结构，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了收敛速度。

Abstract: Various statistical tasks, including sampling or computing Wasserstein barycenters, can be reformulated as fixed-point problems for operators on probability distributions.
  Accelerating standard fixed-point iteration schemes provides a promising novel approach to the design of efficient numerical methods for these problems.
  The Wasserstein geometry on the space of probability measures, although not precisely Riemannian, allows us to define various useful Riemannian notions, such as tangent spaces, exponential maps and parallel transport, motivating the adaptation of Riemannian numerical methods.
  We demonstrate this by developing and implementing the Riemannian Anderson Mixing (RAM) method for Gaussian distributions.
  The method reuses the history of the residuals and improves the iteration complexity, and we argue that the additional costs, compared to Picard method, are negligible.
  We show that certain open balls in the Bures-Wasserstein manifold satisfy the requirements for convergence of RAM.
  The numerical experiments show a significant acceleration compared to a Picard iteration, and performance on par with Riemannian Gradient Descent and Conjugate Gradient methods.

</details>


### [142] [An Invitation to Higher-Order Riemannian Optimization: Optimal and Implementable Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22126)
*David Huckleberry Gutman,George Lobo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在黎曼流形上寻找非凸光滑目标函数一阶和二阶驻点的首个最优p阶方法(p≥1)，证明了流形上非凸优化的最优oracle复杂度与欧氏空间匹配，并建立了研究流形上高阶正则性的系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优算法复杂度，填补现有研究在测地凸假设下的局限，建立流形上非凸优化的理论基础和高效算法框架。

Method: 提出p阶最优方法(p≥1)，引入基于回缩(pullback)连接和Sasaki度量的系统框架研究流形上的高阶正则性，推导新的协变Faà di Bruno公式，对于p=3的情况，通过新的Krylov框架最小化四次正则化三次多项式。

Result: 首次证明黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优oracle复杂度与欧氏空间匹配，建立了研究流形上高阶正则性的首个系统框架，提出了首个用于最小化四次正则化三次多项式的Krylov方法。

Conclusion: 该研究在黎曼流形非凸优化领域取得了突破性进展，不仅建立了最优算法复杂度理论，还提供了系统的高阶正则性分析框架和可实现的算法，为流形优化开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: This paper presents the first optimal-rate $p$-th order methods with $p\geq 1$ for finding first and second-order stationary points of non-convex smooth objective functions over Riemannian manifolds. In contrast to the geodesically convex setting, we definitively establish that the optimal oracle complexity of non-convex optimization over manifolds matches that over Euclidean space. In parallel with the complexity analysis, we introduce a general framework for systematically studying higher-order regularity on Riemannian manifolds that characterizes its joint dependence on the objective function and the chosen retraction. To the best of our knowledge, this framework constitutes the first known application in optimization of pullback connections and the Sasaki metric to the study of retraction-based pullbacks of the objective function. We provide clean derivative bounds based on a new covariant Faà di Bruno formula derived within our framework. For $p=3$, our methods are fully implementable via a new Krylov-based framework for minimizing quartically regularized cubic polynomials. This is the first Krylov method for this class of polynomials and may be of independent interest beyond Riemannian optimization.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Rethinking LLM-Driven Heuristic Design: Generating Efficient and Specialized Solvers via Dynamics-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20868)
*Rongzheng Wang,Yihong Huang,Muquan Li,Jiakai Li,Di Liang,Bob Simons,Pei Ke,Shuang Liang,Ke Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DASH框架通过收敛感知指标联合优化求解器搜索机制和运行时调度，并引入档案库检索实现高效热启动，显著提升组合优化问题的求解效率和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的启发式设计框架存在两个关键限制：1) 仅基于最终质量评估求解器，忽略收敛过程和运行时效率；2) 分布偏移时需要昂贵的重新适应成本。需要一种能同时优化求解效率和适应成本的新框架。

Method: 提出DASH框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 基于收敛感知指标的联合优化机制，共同优化求解器搜索机制和运行时调度；2) 档案库检索(PLR)，在进化过程中并行归档专用求解器，实现异构分布下的低成本热启动。

Result: 在四个组合优化问题上的实验表明：DASH将运行时效率提升3倍以上，并在不同问题规模上超越最先进基线的解质量。通过基于档案的热启动，在不同分布下保持优越精度的同时，将LLM适应成本降低90%以上。

Conclusion: DASH框架通过收敛感知优化和档案库检索机制，有效解决了现有LLM驱动启发式设计框架的局限性，实现了求解器效率、质量和适应成本的多方面提升，为组合优化问题的自动化求解提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced the field of Combinatorial Optimization through automated heuristic generation. Instead of relying on manual design, this LLM-Driven Heuristic Design (LHD) process leverages LLMs to iteratively generate and refine solvers to achieve high performance. However, existing LHD frameworks face two critical limitations: (1) Endpoint-only evaluation, which ranks solvers solely by final quality, ignoring the convergence process and runtime efficiency; (2) High adaptation costs, where distribution shifts necessitate re-adaptation to generate specialized solvers for new instance groups. To address these issues, we propose Dynamics-Aware Solver Heuristics (DASH), a framework that co-optimizes solver search mechanisms and runtime schedules guided by a convergence-aware metric, thereby identifying efficient and high-performance solvers. Furthermore, to mitigate expensive re-adaptation, DASH incorporates Profiled Library Retrieval (PLR). PLR efficiently archives specialized solvers concurrently with the evolutionary process, enabling cost-effective warm-starts for heterogeneous distributions. Experiments on four combinatorial optimization problems demonstrate that DASH improves runtime efficiency by over 3 times, while surpassing the solution quality of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse problem scales. Furthermore, by enabling profile-based warm starts, DASH maintains superior accuracy under different distributions while cutting LLM adaptation costs by over 90%.

</details>


### [144] [Finetune-Informed Pretraining Boosts Downstream Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20884)
*Atik Faysal,Mohammad Rostami,Reihaneh Gh. Roshan,Nikhil Muralidhar,Huaxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Finetune-Informed Pretraining (FIP)方法，在预训练阶段偏向目标模态，以优化下游单模态微调性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态预训练通常均匀处理所有模态，但在实际部署中，下游微调往往只使用单一模态，导致对实际重要模态的表征学习不足

Method: FIP通过为目标模态设置更高的掩码难度、更强的损失权重和更大的解码器容量，在不修改共享编码器或增加额外监督的情况下，使表征学习偏向目标模态

Result: 在无线信号星座图的掩码建模任务中，FIP能持续提升下游微调性能，无需额外数据或计算资源

Conclusion: FIP是一种简单易实现、架构兼容且广泛适用于多模态掩码建模流程的方法，能有效优化针对特定目标模态的表征学习

Abstract: Multimodal pretraining is effective for building general-purpose representations, but in many practical deployments, only one modality is heavily used during downstream fine-tuning. Standard pretraining strategies treat all modalities uniformly, which can lead to under-optimized representations for the modality that actually matters. We propose Finetune-Informed Pretraining (FIP), a model-agnostic method that biases representation learning toward a designated target modality needed at fine-tuning time. FIP combines higher masking difficulty, stronger loss weighting, and increased decoder capacity for the target modality, without modifying the shared encoder or requiring additional supervision. When applied to masked modeling on constellation diagrams for wireless signals, FIP consistently improves downstream fine-tuned performance with no extra data or compute. FIP is simple to implement, architecture-compatible, and broadly applicable across multimodal masked modeling pipelines.

</details>


### [145] [A generative machine learning model for designing metal hydrides applied to hydrogen storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20892)
*Xiyuan Liu,Christian Hacker,Shengnian Wang,Yuhua Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合因果发现与轻量级生成机器学习模型的框架，用于生成新型金属氢化物候选材料，扩展氢存储数据集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料数据库（如Materials Project）中表征良好的氢化物数量有限，限制了最优候选材料的发现，需要开发新方法来扩展氢存储材料数据集

Method: 集成因果发现与轻量级生成机器学习模型，使用450个样本数据集（270训练、90验证、90测试），生成1000个候选材料，通过排序和筛选识别新化学式和晶体结构

Result: 识别出6个先前未报道的化学式和晶体结构，其中4个通过密度泛函理论模拟验证，显示出未来实验研究的强大潜力

Conclusion: 该框架为扩展氢存储数据集和加速材料发现提供了可扩展且时间高效的方法

Abstract: Developing new metal hydrides is a critical step toward efficient hydrogen storage in carbon-neutral energy systems. However, existing materials databases, such as the Materials Project, contain a limited number of well-characterized hydrides, which constrains the discovery of optimal candidates. This work presents a framework that integrates causal discovery with a lightweight generative machine learning model to generate novel metal hydride candidates that may not exist in current databases. Using a dataset of 450 samples (270 training, 90 validation, and 90 testing), the model generates 1,000 candidates. After ranking and filtering, six previously unreported chemical formulas and crystal structures are identified, four of which are validated by density functional theory simulations and show strong potential for future experimental investigation. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and time-efficient approach for expanding hydrogen storage datasets and accelerating materials discovery.

</details>


### [146] [Is Parameter Isolation Better for Prompt-Based Continual Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20894)
*Jiangyang Li,Chenhao Ding,Songlin Dong,Qiang Wang,Jianchao Zhao,Yuhang He,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于提示共享的持续学习框架，通过全局提示池和任务感知门控路由机制实现动态解耦与协作优化，解决传统方法提示固定分配导致的参数利用不足和知识隔离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的持续学习方法通常为每个任务分配固定提示集，导致任务间知识完全隔离、参数利用效率低下，无法满足实际持续学习需求。

Method: 构建全局提示池，引入任务感知门控路由机制稀疏激活提示子集，实现任务特定特征表示的动态解耦与协作优化；设计历史感知调制器，利用累积提示激活统计保护常用提示免受过量更新。

Result: 广泛分析和实证结果表明，该方法在效果和效率上持续优于现有静态分配策略。

Conclusion: 提出的提示共享框架通过动态路由和调制机制有效解决了传统固定提示分配方法的局限性，实现了更高效的参数利用和知识保留。

Abstract: Prompt-based continual learning methods effectively mitigate catastrophic forgetting. However, most existing methods assign a fixed set of prompts to each task, completely isolating knowledge across tasks and resulting in suboptimal parameter utilization. To address this, we consider the practical needs of continual learning and propose a prompt-sharing framework. This framework constructs a global prompt pool and introduces a task-aware gated routing mechanism that sparsely activates a subset of prompts to achieve dynamic decoupling and collaborative optimization of task-specific feature representations. Furthermore, we introduce a history-aware modulator that leverages cumulative prompt activation statistics to protect frequently used prompts from excessive updates, thereby mitigating inefficient parameter usage and knowledge forgetting. Extensive analysis and empirical results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing static allocation strategies in effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [147] [TwinWeaver: An LLM-Based Foundation Model Framework for Pan-Cancer Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20906)
*Nikita Makarov,Maria Bordukova,Lena Voith von Voithenberg,Estrella Pivel-Villanueva,Sabrina Mielke,Jonathan Wickes,Hanchen Wang,Mingyu Derek Ma,Keunwoo Choi,Kyunghyun Cho,Stephen Ra,Raul Rodriguez-Esteban,Fabian Schmich,Michael Menden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TwinWeaver框架将患者纵向历史序列化为文本，使大语言模型能统一进行事件预测和预后预测，构建的Genie Digital Twin在癌症患者数据上显著优于传统时间序列方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要预测临床事件和病程轨迹，但稀疏、多模态临床时间序列建模仍是一个关键挑战。现有方法难以有效处理这种复杂数据。

Method: 提出TwinWeaver开源框架，将纵向患者历史序列化为文本，使大语言模型能统一进行事件预测和预后预测。基于该框架构建Genie Digital Twin（GDT），在93,054名患者、20种癌症类型的数据上进行训练。

Result: GDT显著降低预测误差，中位MASE为0.87（基线0.97，p<0.001）。在生存、进展和治疗转换任务中平均C-index达0.703（最佳基线0.662）。在分布外临床试验数据上，零样本匹配训练基线，微调后表现更优（中位MASE 0.75-0.88，平均C-index 0.672 vs 0.648）。

Conclusion: TwinWeaver为纵向临床建模提供了可扩展且透明的基础，其可解释的临床推理扩展增强了框架的实用性，展示了LLM在临床时间序列预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Precision oncology requires forecasting clinical events and trajectories, yet modeling sparse, multi-modal clinical time series remains a critical challenge. We introduce TwinWeaver, an open-source framework that serializes longitudinal patient histories into text, enabling unified event prediction as well as forecasting with large language models, and use it to build Genie Digital Twin (GDT) on 93,054 patients across 20 cancer types. In benchmarks, GDT significantly reduces forecasting error, achieving a median Mean Absolute Scaled Error (MASE) of 0.87 compared to 0.97 for the strongest time-series baseline (p<0.001). Furthermore, GDT improves risk stratification, achieving an average concordance index (C-index) of 0.703 across survival, progression, and therapy switching tasks, surpassing the best baseline of 0.662. GDT also generalizes to out-of-distribution clinical trials, matching trained baselines at zero-shot and surpassing them with fine-tuning, achieving a median MASE of 0.75-0.88 and outperforming the strongest baseline in event prediction with an average C-index of 0.672 versus 0.648. Finally, TwinWeaver enables an interpretable clinical reasoning extension, providing a scalable and transparent foundation for longitudinal clinical modeling.

</details>


### [148] [Noisy but Valid: Robust Statistical Evaluation of LLMs with Imperfect Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20913)
*Chen Feng,Minghe Shen,Ananth Balashankar,Carsten Gerner-Beuerle,Miguel R. D. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个"有噪声但有效"的假设检验框架，用于在LLM评估者存在不完美、噪声和偏差的情况下，可靠地认证大语言模型的安全阈值，同时保证有限样本的I类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估者虽然具有可扩展性，但评估者的不完美性、噪声和偏差会破坏统计保证。需要一种能够处理评估者缺陷的可靠认证方法。

Method: 使用少量人工标注的校准集估计评估者的真阳性率和假阳性率，然后将方差校正的临界阈值应用于大量评估者标注的数据集，构建一个理论上保证有限样本I类错误控制的假设检验框架。

Result: 1) 推导出噪声测试比直接评估具有更高统计功效的确切条件；2) 在Jigsaw Comment、Hate Speech和SafeRLHF数据集上的实验验证了理论；3) 揭示了实际方法与理论"Oracle"之间的显著性能差距，量化了估计成本。

Conclusion: 该框架首次系统处理了不完美评估者设置，提供了评估者可靠性的可解释诊断，阐明了评估功效如何依赖于评估者质量、数据集大小和认证水平，为使用LLM评估者进行统计评估提供了更清晰的理解。

Abstract: Reliable certification of Large Language Models (LLMs)-verifying that failure rates are below a safety threshold-is critical yet challenging. While "LLM-as-a-Judge" offers scalability, judge imperfections, noise, and bias can invalidate statistical guarantees. We introduce a "Noisy but Valid" hypothesis testing framework to address this. By leveraging a small human-labelled calibration set to estimate the judge's True Positive and False Positive Rates (TPR/FPR), we derive a variance-corrected critical threshold applied to a large judge-labelled dataset. Crucially, our framework theoretically guarantees finite-sample Type-I error control (validity) despite calibration uncertainty. This distinguishes our work from Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI), positioning our method as a diagnostic tool that explicitly models judge behavior rather than a black-box estimator. Our contributions include: (1) Theoretical Guarantees: We derive the exact conditions under which noisy testing yields higher statistical power than direct evaluation; (2) Empirical Validation: Experiments on Jigsaw Comment, Hate Speech and SafeRLHF confirm our theory; (3) The Oracle Gap: We reveal a significant performance gap between practical methods and the theoretical "Oracle" (perfectly known judge parameters), quantifying the cost of estimation. Specifically, we provide the first systematic treatment of the imperfect-judge setting, yielding interpretable diagnostics of judge reliability and clarifying how evaluation power depends on judge quality, dataset size, and certification levels. Together, these results sharpen understanding of statistical evaluation with LLM judges, and highlight trade-offs among competing inferential tools.

</details>


### [149] [Noninvasive Intracranial Pressure Estimation Using Subspace System Identification and Bespoke Machine Learning Algorithms: A Learning-to-Rank Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20916)
*Anni Zhao,Ayca Ermis,Jeffrey Robert Vitt,Sergio Brasil,Wellingson Paiva,Magdalena Kasprowicz,Malgorzata Burzynska,Robert Hamilton,Runze Yan,Ofer Sadan,J. Claude Hemphill,Lieven Vandenberghe,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合系统辨识和排序约束优化的机器学习算法，利用无创信号（动脉血压、脑血流速度、R-R间期）估计颅内压，约31.88%测试样本误差在2mmHg内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确无创估计颅内压（ICP）是重症监护领域的重要挑战，现有方法存在局限性，需要开发更精确的无创ICP监测技术。

Method: 1) 使用子空间系统辨识算法，基于动脉血压、脑血流速度和R-R间期信号识别脑血流动力学模型进行ICP模拟；2) 通过凸优化学习无创信号特征与估计误差之间的映射函数，采用创新的排序约束；3) 将多临床环境患者随机分为训练集和测试集进行性能评估。

Result: 测试结果显示：约31.88%的测试样本估计误差在2mmHg以内，约34.07%的测试样本误差在2-6mmHg之间，证明了该无创ICP估计方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的无创ICP估计方法的可行性，为急性脑损伤及相关疾病患者的安全、广泛可及的ICP监测奠定了基础，但需要进一步验证和技术改进才能临床部署。

Abstract: Objective: Accurate noninvasive estimation of intracranial pressure (ICP) remains a major challenge in critical care. We developed a bespoke machine learning algorithm that integrates system identification and ranking-constrained optimization to estimate mean ICP from noninvasive signals. Methods: A machine learning framework was proposed to obtain accurate mean ICP values using arbitrary noninvasive signals. The subspace system identification algorithm is employed to identify cerebral hemodynamics models for ICP simulation using arterial blood pressure (ABP), cerebral blood velocity (CBv), and R-wave to R-wave interval (R-R interval) signals in a comprehensive database. A mapping function to describe the relationship between the features of noninvasive signals and the estimation errors is learned using innovative ranking constraints through convex optimization. Patients across multiple clinical settings were randomly split into testing and training datasets for performance evaluation of the mapping function. Results: The results indicate that about 31.88% of testing entries achieved estimation errors within 2 mmHg and 34.07% of testing entries between 2 mmHg to 6 mmHg from the nonlinear mapping with constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed noninvasive ICP estimation approach. Significance: Further validation and technical refinement are required before clinical deployment, but this work lays the foundation for safe and broadly accessible ICP monitoring in patients with acute brain injury and related conditions.

</details>


### [150] [A Theory of Universal Agnostic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20961)
*Steve Hanneke,Shay Moran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了不可知设置下二分类问题的最优通用速率完整理论，将可实情况理论扩展到不可知设置，识别出最优速率的四分类


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Bousquet等人(2021)的可实情况理论，去除分布的可实现性假设，建立不可知设置下二分类的最优通用速率完整理论

Method: 识别出最优速率的四分类：e^{-n}、e^{-o(n)}、o(n^{-1/2})和任意慢速，并确定决定概念类属于哪一类的简单组合结构

Result: 建立了不可知设置下二分类最优通用速率的完整理论，证明了每个概念类的最优收敛速率必定属于四类之一，并找到了决定分类的组合结构

Conclusion: 该研究为不可知设置下的二分类问题提供了完整的理论框架，揭示了最优速率的四分类结构，为理解学习算法的极限性能提供了理论基础

Abstract: We provide a complete theory of optimal universal rates for binary classification in the agnostic setting. This extends the realizable-case theory of Bousquet, Hanneke, Moran, van Handel, and Yehudayoff (2021) by removing the realizability assumption on the distribution. We identify a fundamental tetrachotomy of optimal rates: for every concept class, the optimal universal rate of convergence of the excess error rate is one of $e^{-n}$, $e^{-o(n)}$, $o(n^{-1/2})$, or arbitrarily slow. We further identify simple combinatorial structures which determine which of these categories any given concept class falls into.

</details>


### [151] [Distributional Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20985)
*Abdullah Akgül,Gulcin Baykal,Manuel Haußmann,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将主动推理整合到分布强化学习框架中，无需建模转移动态即可获得性能优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在复杂环境中的最优控制面临两个互补且交织的挑战：感官状态信息的有效组织和远见行动规划。强化学习仅解决后者，导致样本效率低下。主动推理是解释生物大脑如何处理这一双重问题的最先进过程理论，但其在人工智能中的应用仅限于现有基于模型方法的扩展。

Method: 提出一个形式化的强化学习算法抽象，涵盖基于模型、分布式和免模型方法。该抽象将主动推理无缝集成到分布强化学习框架中，无需转移动态建模即可利用其性能优势。

Result: 该方法使主动推理的性能优势在无需显式建模环境转移动态的情况下变得可访问，解决了传统强化学习样本效率低下的问题。

Conclusion: 通过将主动推理整合到分布强化学习框架中，该工作为复杂环境中的机器人控制提供了更高效的解决方案，既保留了主动推理的理论优势，又避免了传统基于模型方法对转移动态建模的需求。

Abstract: Optimal control of complex environments with robotic systems faces two complementary and intertwined challenges: efficient organization of sensory state information and far-sighted action planning. Because the reinforcement learning framework addresses only the latter, it tends to deliver sample-inefficient solutions. Active inference is the state-of-the-art process theory that explains how biological brains handle this dual problem. However, its applications to artificial intelligence have thus far been limited to extensions of existing model-based approaches. We present a formal abstraction of reinforcement learning algorithms that spans model-based, distributional, and model-free approaches. This abstraction seamlessly integrates active inference into the distributional reinforcement learning framework, making its performance advantages accessible without transition dynamics modeling.

</details>


### [152] [Pre-trained Encoders for Global Child Development: Transfer Learning Enables Deployment in Data-Scarce Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20987)
*Md Muhtasim Munif Fahim,Md Rezaul Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发首个全球儿童发展预训练编码器，使用UNICEF 44国35.7万儿童数据训练，仅需50个样本即可实现0.65 AUC，显著优于冷启动方法，支持资源受限环境下的SDG 4.2.1监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量儿童每年经历可预防的发展迟缓，但机器学习在新国家的部署面临数据瓶颈：可靠模型需要数千样本，而新项目通常只有不到100个样本。

Method: 引入首个全球儿童发展预训练编码器，使用UNICEF调查数据在44个国家357,709名儿童上进行训练。采用迁移学习框架，应用转移学习边界解释预训练多样性如何实现少样本泛化。

Result: 仅需50个训练样本，预训练编码器平均AUC达0.65（95% CI: 0.56-0.72），比冷启动梯度提升的0.61提高8-12%。500个样本时AUC达0.73。零样本部署到未见国家AUC最高达0.84。

Conclusion: 预训练编码器能够显著改变资源受限环境下机器学习用于SDG 4.2.1监测的可行性，通过预训练多样性实现少样本泛化。

Abstract: A large number of children experience preventable developmental delays each year, yet the deployment of machine learning in new countries has been stymied by a data bottleneck: reliable models require thousands of samples, while new programs begin with fewer than 100. We introduce the first pre-trained encoder for global child development, trained on 357,709 children across 44 countries using UNICEF survey data. With only 50 training samples, the pre-trained encoder achieves an average AUC of 0.65 (95% CI: 0.56-0.72), outperforming cold-start gradient boosting at 0.61 by 8-12% across regions. At N=500, the encoder achieves an AUC of 0.73. Zero-shot deployment to unseen countries achieves AUCs up to 0.84. We apply a transfer learning bound to explain why pre-training diversity enables few-shot generalization. These results establish that pre-trained encoders can transform the feasibility of ML for SDG 4.2.1 monitoring in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [153] [Top-k on a Budget: Adaptive Ranking with Weak and Strong Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20989)
*Lutz Oettershagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在弱/强双预言机设置下的top-k识别问题，提出自适应认证算法ACE和两阶段方法ACE-W，显著减少对昂贵强预言机的调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别top-k项目是基础任务，但当精确评估代价高昂时变得困难。实际应用中常存在快速但噪声的弱预言机（如近似模型）和稀缺但高保真的强预言机（如人类专家验证或昂贵模拟）。需要设计算法在保证准确性的同时最小化对昂贵强预言机的依赖。

Method: 1. 分析基线方法STC（筛选后认证），证明其在弱置信区间最大半径ε_max下最多需要m(4ε_max)次强调用。2. 提出ACE算法，自适应地将强查询集中在关键边界项目上，达到相同的O(m(4ε_max))理论界但实践中显著减少强调用。3. 提出ACE-W两阶段方法，先自适应分配弱预算，再运行ACE，进一步降低强成本。

Result: 1. 建立了条件下限Ω(m(ε_max))，表明任何算法在相同弱不确定性下都需要至少这么多强调用。2. ACE算法在理论上达到与STC相同的O(m(4ε_max))上界，但在实践中显著减少强调用次数。3. ACE-W通过自适应弱预算分配进一步降低了强预言机成本。

Conclusion: 论文为双预言机设置下的top-k识别提供了理论分析和实用算法。ACE和ACE-W算法在保证准确性的同时，通过自适应地将资源集中在关键项目上，显著减少了对昂贵强预言机的依赖，为解决实际应用中评估成本高昂的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Identifying the top-$k$ items is fundamental but often prohibitive when exact valuations are expensive. We study a two-oracle setting with a fast, noisy weak oracle and a scarce, high-fidelity strong oracle (e.g., human expert verification or expensive simulation). We first analyze a simple screen-then-certify baseline (STC) and prove it makes at most $m(4\varepsilon_{\max})$ strong calls given jointly valid weak confidence intervals with maximum radius $\varepsilon_{\max}$, where $m(\cdot)$ denotes the near-tie mass around the top-$k$ threshold. We establish a conditional lower bound of $Ω(m(\varepsilon_{\max}))$ for any algorithm given the same weak uncertainty. Our main contribution is ACE, an adaptive certification algorithm that focuses strong queries on critical boundary items, achieving the same $O(m(4\varepsilon_{\max}))$ bound while reducing strong calls in practice. We then introduce ACE-W, a fully adaptive two-phase method that allocates weak budget adaptively before running ACE, further reducing strong costs.

</details>


### [154] [The Depth Delusion: Why Transformers Should Be Wider, Not Deeper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20994)
*Md Muhtasim Munif Fahim,Md Rezaul Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了架构条件化的缩放定律，发现宽度应比深度增长更快（2.8倍），并揭示了"深度错觉"现象：超过临界深度后，增加层数反而会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经缩放定律将架构视为可互换的，但实际中不同架构（深度vs宽度）对性能有显著影响。需要理解架构参数（深度和宽度）如何独立影响模型缩放，以指导实际架构设计。

Method: 提出架构条件化的缩放定律，将损失函数分解为对深度和宽度的依赖关系。通过30个transformer架构（1700万到70亿参数）的实验验证，每个架构在代表性高计算样本上训练。

Result: 发现最优深度缩放为D* ~ C^0.12，最优宽度缩放为W* ~ C^0.34，宽度增长应比深度快2.8倍。揭示了临界深度现象：超过D_crit ~ W^0.44后，增加层数反而增加损失（深度错觉）。在70亿参数规模下，64层模型（63.8亿参数）比32层模型（68.6亿参数）性能差0.12 nats。

Conclusion: 深度-宽度权衡在工业规模下仍然存在，架构设计不能简单视为可互换。宽度应比深度增长更快，超过临界深度会导致性能下降，这对大规模语言模型的实际设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how language model loss decreases with parameters and data, but treat architecture as interchangeable--a billion parameters could arise from a shallow-wide model (10 layers & 8,192 hidden dimension) or a deep-narrow one (80 layers & 2,048 hidden dimension). We propose architecture-conditioned scaling laws decomposing this dependence, finding that optimal depth scales as D* ~ C^0.12 while optimal width scales as W* ~ C^0.34, meaning width should grow 2.8x faster than depth. We discover a critical depth phenomenon: beyond D_crit ~ W^0.44 (sublinear in W), adding layers increases loss despite adding parameters--the Depth Delusion. Empirically, we validate these findings across 30 transformer architectures spanning 17M to 7B parameters, each trained on representative high-compute samples, achieving R^2 = 0.922. Our central finding: at 7B scale, a 64-layer model (6.38B params) underperforms a 32-layer model (6.86B params) by 0.12 nats, despite being significantly deeper. This demonstrates that optimal depth-width tradeoffs persist at the production scale.

</details>


### [155] [Solver-in-the-Loop: MDP-Based Benchmarks for Self-Correction and Behavioral Rationality in Operations Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21008)
*Ruicheng Ao,David Simchi-Levi,Xinshang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两个新的运筹学基准测试ORDebug和ORBias，将求解器纳入评估循环，用于评估LLM在迭代调试和决策偏差方面的能力，发现针对性的训练方法可以超越规模效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试将运筹学问题视为一次性代码生成任务，忽略了实际应用中通过分析不可行子系统(IIS)进行迭代调试的诊断循环过程，缺乏对模型在实际求解环境中表现的真实评估。

Method: 提出两个新基准：1) ORDebug：包含5000+个问题，涵盖9种错误类型，评估迭代自我修正能力，每次修复后重新执行求解器并重新计算IIS；2) ORBias：包含2000个报童问题实例，评估行为理性，测量与闭式最优策略的系统性偏差。对26个模型进行12000+样本测试。

Result: 领域特定的RLVR训练使8B模型超越前沿API：恢复率95.3% vs 86.2%（+9.1%），诊断准确率62.4% vs 47.8%（+14.6%），解决步骤2.25 vs 3.78（快1.7倍）。在ORBias上，课程训练实现了唯一的负ID→OOD偏差漂移（-9.6%），系统性偏差减少48%（从20.0%到10.4%）。

Conclusion: 通过可验证的求解器反馈进行过程级评估，使得针对性的训练方法能够超越单纯模型规模的扩展，为运筹学领域的LLM评估提供了更真实的测试框架。

Abstract: Operations Research practitioners routinely debug infeasible models through an iterative process: analyzing Irreducible Infeasible Subsystems (\IIS{}), identifying constraint conflicts, and systematically repairing formulations until feasibility is achieved. Yet existing LLM benchmarks evaluate OR as one-shot translation -- given a problem description, generate solver code -- ignoring this diagnostic loop entirely. We introduce two benchmarks that place the \textbf{solver in the evaluation loop}. \textbf{\ORDebug{}} evaluates iterative self-correction through 5,000+ problems spanning 9 error types; each repair action triggers solver re-execution and \IIS{} recomputation, providing deterministic, verifiable feedback. \textbf{\ORBias{}} evaluates behavioral rationality through 2,000 newsvendor instances (1,000 ID + 1,000 OOD), measuring systematic deviations from closed-form optimal policies. Across 26 models and 12,000+ samples, we find that domain-specific RLVR training enables an 8B model to surpass frontier APIs: 95.3\% vs 86.2\% recovery rate (+9.1\%), 62.4\% vs 47.8\% diagnostic accuracy (+14.6\%), and 2.25 vs 3.78 steps to resolution (1.7$\times$ faster). On \ORBias{}, curriculum training achieves the only negative ID$\rightarrow$OOD bias drift among models evaluated (-9.6\%), reducing systematic bias by 48\% (from 20.0\% to 10.4\%). These results demonstrate that process-level evaluation with verifiable oracles enables targeted training that outperforms scale.

</details>


### [156] [Order-Aware Test-Time Adaptation: Leveraging Temporal Dynamics for Robust Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21012)
*Young Kyung Kim,Oded Schlesinger,Qiangqiang Wu,J. Matías Di Martino,Guillermo Sapiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OATTA是一种利用时间动态进行测试时适应的新方法，通过贝叶斯估计和时间先验提升模型在分布偏移下的性能，同时引入安全机制防止错误适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法通常将测试流视为独立样本，忽略了时间动态中固有的监督信号，这限制了模型在分布偏移下的适应能力。

Method: 将测试时适应建模为无梯度递归贝叶斯估计任务，使用学习到的动态转移矩阵作为时间先验来优化基础模型的预测，并引入似然比门控机制确保在缺乏时间证据时回退到基础预测器。

Result: 在图像分类、可穿戴和生理信号分析、语言情感分析等多个领域的实验中，OATTA一致提升了现有基线的性能，最高可将准确率提高6.35%，证明了其通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 建模时间动态为测试时适应提供了超越标准顺序无关方法的关键正交信号，OATTA作为一种轻量级、模型无关的模块，在计算开销可忽略的情况下显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) enables pre-trained models to adjust to distribution shift by learning from unlabeled test-time streams. However, existing methods typically treat these streams as independent samples, overlooking the supervisory signal inherent in temporal dynamics. To address this, we introduce Order-Aware Test-Time Adaptation (OATTA). We formulate test-time adaptation as a gradient-free recursive Bayesian estimation task, using a learned dynamic transition matrix as a temporal prior to refine the base model's predictions. To ensure safety in weakly structured streams, we introduce a likelihood-ratio gate (LLR) that reverts to the base predictor when temporal evidence is absent. OATTA is a lightweight, model-agnostic module that incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments across image classification, wearable and physiological signal analysis, and language sentiment analysis demonstrate its universality; OATTA consistently boosts established baselines, improving accuracy by up to 6.35%. Our findings establish that modeling temporal dynamics provides a critical, orthogonal signal beyond standard order-agnostic TTA approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Perceptrons and localization of attention's mean-field landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21366)
*Antonio Álvarez-López,Borjan Geshkovski,Domènec Ruiz-Balet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将Transformer前向传播建模为球面上的粒子系统，研究感知器块对系统临界点的影响，发现临界点通常是原子化的且集中在球面子集上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Transformer的前向传播视为单位球面上的相互作用粒子系统，其中时间对应层数，粒子对应词嵌入，单位球面理想化了层归一化。研究在这种框架下感知器块对系统动力学的影响，特别是临界点的结构特性。

Method: 将Transformer前向传播建模为球面上的粒子系统，利用Wasserstein梯度流理论分析无限上下文长度（平均场）极限。在特定权重设置下，系统可视为显式能量的梯度流。重点研究感知器块在这种设置下的作用。

Result: 研究发现，在这种粒子系统框架下，临界点通常是原子化的（atomic），并且集中在球面的子集上，表现出局部化特征。

Conclusion: 感知器块在Transformer的粒子系统表示中起着重要作用，导致临界点呈现原子化和局部化结构，这为理解Transformer的动力学行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The forward pass of a Transformer can be seen as an interacting particle system on the unit sphere: time plays the role of layers, particles that of token embeddings, and the unit sphere idealizes layer normalization. In some weight settings the system can even be seen as a gradient flow for an explicit energy, and one can make sense of the infinite context length (mean-field) limit thanks to Wasserstein gradient flows. In this paper we study the effect of the perceptron block in this setting, and show that critical points are generically atomic and localized on subsets of the sphere.

</details>


### [158] [Predict-Project-Renoise: Sampling Diffusion Models under Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21033)
*Omer Rochman-Sharabi,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PPR（Predict-Project-Renoise）框架，在扩散模型采样过程中通过预测-投影-再噪声的迭代循环强制满足物理约束，显著提升科学应用中的物理准确性和约束满足度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的神经模拟器在科学应用中具有潜力，但传统模型无法保证物理准确性或约束满足。需要一种方法在生成时强制执行硬约束（如物理定律或观测一致性）

Method: 提出约束采样框架：1）定义约束前向过程，仅在满足约束的可行集上进行扩散；2）提出PPR（Predict-Project-Renoise）算法，通过迭代的预测（去噪）、投影（到可行集）、再噪声步骤从约束边缘分布中采样

Result: 在2D分布、偏微分方程和全球天气预报任务上的实验表明，PPR将约束违反降低了超过一个数量级，同时改善了样本一致性，更好地匹配真实的约束分布

Conclusion: PPR框架能够有效将硬约束整合到扩散模型采样过程中，显著提升科学应用中神经模拟器的物理准确性和约束满足能力，为约束感知的生成建模提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Neural emulators based on diffusion models show promise for scientific applications, but vanilla models cannot guarantee physical accuracy or constraint satisfaction. We address this by introducing a constrained sampling framework that enforces hard constraints, such as physical laws or observational consistency, at generation time. Our approach defines a constrained forward process that diffuses only over the feasible set of constraint-satisfying samples, inducing constrained marginal distributions. To reverse this, we propose Predict-Project-Renoise (PPR), an iterative algorithm that samples from the constrained marginals by alternating between denoising predictions, projecting onto the feasible set, and renoising. Experiments on 2D distributions, PDEs, and global weather forecasting demonstrate that PPR reduces constraint violations by over an order of magnitude while improving sample consistency and better matching the true constrained distribution compared to baselines.

</details>


### [159] [A block-coordinate descent framework for non-convex composite optimization. Application to sparse precision matrix estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21467)
*Guillaume Lauga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个新的块坐标下降（BCD）框架用于非凸复合优化问题，确保目标函数下降并收敛到解，该框架能包含多种更新方法，在稀疏精度矩阵估计问题上显著减少迭代次数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块坐标下降（BCD）方法在大规模优化问题中被广泛使用，但其在非凸优化中的理论研究相对不足，需要一个新的通用框架来确保收敛性并提高效率

Method: 提出一个新的块坐标下降（BCD）框架，能够包含变量度量近端梯度更新、近端牛顿更新和交替最小化更新，该框架足够通用以涵盖图形LASSO问题中最常用的三种求解器

Result: 在非凸稀疏精度矩阵估计问题上，该框架提供了收敛保证，并且相比现有方法，达到最先进估计质量所需的迭代次数最多减少了100倍

Conclusion: 新的BCD框架为处理非凸复合优化问题提供了一个通用且有效的解决方案，特别在图形LASSO问题上展现出显著的计算优势
